# Session AL — Grok Alpha #53 ledger (GROK53_LEDGER)

Build 17069c7d (Alpha #53), Grok bot run 4 Nov 2026 ≈ 01:00–05:50 Brussels. Inputs: the eight per-bot ledgers in `docs/review/session-al/ledger/` (berlin, denver, denver-recheck-gaps, lisbon, moscow, nairobi, professor-crosspage-oldids, tokyo). Keys: `ROOT_CAUSE_KEYS.md`. Rules: R1 (one line per finding), R2 (history), A2 (root-cause keys), A11 (competitor gaps one row each unless two bots name the same feature).

How this file was built: a script parsed every table row of the eight ledgers, mapped each item (finding, old id, prompt, competitor item, copy note) to the P-row in its `P-row` cell, and merged P-rows into AL rows by an explicit, reviewed merge list (one fix at one shared source; cross-bot merges by root cause + shared source). Every P-row belongs to exactly one AL row; every id has exactly one primary AL row, or sits on the keep-list, or is NR (no 11-04 evidence: not retested / out of area / retracted).

Verdicts are normalised from each row's "bot verdict" cell (RE-FOUND OPEN → STILL OPEN, RE-FOUND CHANGED → CHANGED-but-wrong, NOT REPRODUCED/OK → FIXED, NOT SEEN/N/A/excluded → NOT RETESTED). Tokyo's rows are its re-check list rows (W1…Z21, F1…F10, pass-1 and in-run lines), each naming several old ids; Professor's §4 rows can name several ids ("N-079 / N-088"). The same old id is often re-checked by more than one bot; the index (d) gives each id one primary AL row.

### Reconciliation check (ids in = ids mapped)

- Item lines parsed: 4033 (findings 1571, old-id rows 1414, prompt rows 537, competitor items 349, copy notes 162). Ids are taken from the first cell; multi-id cells are split; prompt ids are namespaced per bot (`DV:Z-001` is a Denver prompt, `Z-001` an Oct-26 old id).
- Distinct ids in: **3654**. Mapped to an AL row: **2798**; keep-list only: **613**; NR (no 11-04 evidence, nothing to fix yet): **243**. 2798 + 613 + 243 = 3654 ✔.
- Ids whose lines point to more than one AL row (e.g. an old id re-checked by two bots, or a finding whose line names two P-rows): 51. Each has one primary AL row (first mapping in ledger order); the index lists the others after "also".
- Every P-row maps to exactly one AL row (union-find over the merge list; 946 P-rows → 642 AL rows; 0 P-rows unmapped). P-rows that no item line points to: P-BN-46 (closes BN-123 #5/#6), P-MO-97 (MO-111, secondary), P-DV-14 (empty, merged) — kept, their ids shown as "also closes".
- NR breakdown: NOT RETESTED 137, OUT OF AREA 101, — 3, none (retracted) 1, none (present) 1.
- NR ids that are not old ids (could not be mapped, with why): DV:N-000 (screenshot names only, no prompt); C-NB-16 (retracted by the bot (NB-090: Customize has handles)); C-NB-23 (not a gap: limit/quit habits exist (NB-154/156)); N-PR-10 (coverage note (what Professor did not test), no defect); N-SL-07 (LM Studio not installed/tested, no finding).
- Delegated lines ("→ Tokyo/Moscow/… helper") were resolved through the owner's line for the same id (Tokyo via the old ids inside its re-check rows); BUG-002 (Tokyo, not retested) stays NR, BUG-053 resolves to keep.

## (b) AL rows

Sorted by lane, then severity (max over the row's items; GAP = competitor item; prompt-fail = only failing prompts; note = only copy notes; — = no severity on the line). Conditions = union of the items' conditions (widths in px, desktop, theme, profile: fresh / Showcase / shared; time only for date/time rows). Shared source = the lead P-row's source (guesses as the helper wrote them).
